# 教材8

## 🌱 单词 / 词根paint, pict-, pig- 都有"paint：绘画"的含义。

paint
painted
painter

## 🌱 单词 / 词根paint, pict-, pig- 都有"paint：绘画"的含义。

painting
hand-painted
repaint
